My childhood dream comes to life this weekend! I finally join my partners Stan Lee & 
Regina Carpinelli at the Stan Lee's Comikaze at the Los Angeles Convention Center. 
This is for the dreamers, the outcasts, the heroes, the one's that imagined a greater 
future, a magical past, and a fantastic present. This is for you, the FANS! 
Please join me. 
boron Ofir 
STAN LEE'S COMIKAZE-GET YOUR TICKETS TODAY! 
DOMINATING THE L.A. CONVENTION CENTER THIS SEPTEMBER 15th 
and 16th! 
One year later, Comikaze returns to Los Angeles this weekend! 
Stan Lee's Comikaze Expo presented by POW! Entertainment hits the L.A. 
Convention Center on September 15th & 16th. 
Now double the size, AND featuring a Zombie obstacle course, 
Comikaze 2012 will blow your mind! 
EFTA_R1_00830401 
EFTA02158775
--- PAGE 2 ---
Get tix here! http://comikazeexpo.com/tickets/ 
With tickets priced at only $20 a day (Kids under 12 are $FREE!), we've got 
all your Nerd dreams SIGNED, SEALED, AND DELIVERED! 
Celebrities, Creators, More! 
Comikaze will feature appearances by Stan 
Lee, Elvira, Todd McFarlane, Felicia Day, J. 
Scott Campbell, Neil Adams,Linda Blair, Kevin 
Smith, Thomas Jane, Lou Ferrigno, Norman 
Reedus (The Walking Dead, Boondock 
Saints), the original Batman tv show cast, 
including: Adam West, Burt Ward, Julie 
Newmar and more! 
Brave con-goers can test the new Comikaze 
Zombie Apocalypse, a 75,000-sq ft survival 
course infested with flesh-hungry zombies. 
Participants will be chased through a large 
obstacle course, and must crawl, climb, and 
slide, and run their way to survival! 
If you prefer wizards to zombies, then don't 
miss the Harry Potter-style Quidditch 
BRICKLEBERRY PREMIERE! 
Kicking off this weekend, Stan 
Lee's Comikaze will premiere 
Comedy Central's highly 
anticipated new show, 
"Brickleberry", produced by Daniel 
Tosh of Tosh.O, an exclusive sneak-
peek prior to the show's September 
25th debut! 
EFTA_R1_00830402 
EFTA02158776
--- PAGE 3 ---
Tournament, OR meander through 
Comikaze's two museums —
The Elvira Macabre Museum, which houses 
the largest single collection of Elvira 
memorabilia (meet & greet with Elvira) or, The 
Stan Lee Mega Museum, one of the largest 
Marvel collections in existence, featuring Stan 
Lee's personal collection and Marvel movie 
memorabilia. 
(Stan will be there too) 
Additionally, for this weekend only, Activision 
presents Stan Lee's Comikaze Activision 
Arcade, an area that will feature five of 
Activision's newest games — Tony Hawk 
Shred, Deadpool, 007, Transformers Rise of 
Cybertron and The Walking Dead - all the 
most sought-after previews in one place! 
For all the Nerdy, Gory, and Glorious details, 
go here: 
http://comikazeexpo.com/ 
We'll also have panel discussions with topics such as: 
• 
Kevin Smith's "Fatman on Batman," a chat with the original Batman cast Adam West, 
Burt Ward, and incomparable Julie Newman 
• 
Todd McFarlane and Stan Lee discuss the future of entertainment 
• 
Mark Hamill joins Felicia Day and the cast of "The Guild" 
• 
The Nerd Dating Game, hosted by Adrianne Curry 
• 
Thomas Jane (Punisher, Hung) will pit comedians against comic creators during 
"COMICS ON COMICS" 
Comikaze kicks off the weekend with several pre-show events, including Elvira's 
Horror Hunt, an independently-produced horror film festival on Thursday, Sept 
13th at the Vista Theater and a 98.7FM concert (hosted by Stan Lee and Elvira) at 
Club Nokia Sept 14th with the Godfather of Goth, Peter Murphy. 
EFTA_R1_00830403 
EFTA02158777

Comikaze Expo is the largest multimedia pop culture convention in Los Angeles. 
This massive event brings together the very best in comics, gaming, horror, sci-fi, 
fantasy, anime and all other cult programming.
